# Skills · D · part 29

786 entries, design-by-evidence to design-extract. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **design-by-evidence**: Settle design choices with evidence instead of debate — brainstorm options, have the agent poke holes, prototype two (or more) plausible designs in parallel branches/worktrees, compare against explicit criteria, decide, record the decision, and throw the loser away. Use when the user is torn between approaches, asks "which is better", "should we use X or Y", wants a design review or architecture tradeoff, or is about to commit to a durable decision (API contract, data model, dependency, architecture). Frontier Engineering principles 5 and 6. ([giovanna-uchoa/frontier-kit](https://github.com/giovanna-uchoa/frontier-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/design-by-evidence))
- **design-by-stories**: Design or redesign an artifact's shape — a schema, template, data model, config format, note type, or API surface — by modeling its actors and user stories first, then deriving the shape from the stories. One fork at a time, human adjudicates every fork. Distinct from interrogate-model (critiques an existing model for what it can't express), first-principles (elicits needs for a purchase/problem), and spec-flow:draft (contracts a code change for implementation). ([jdh313/jdh-agents](https://github.com/jdh313/jdh-agents/tree/HEAD/marketplaces/codex/plugins/craft/skills/design-by-stories) · 3 copies)
